2026.09.01

1319.TT 東陽 
 邁入傳統旺季 營運逐季回升

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司主要營業項目為汽 、 機車零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第一大汽車AM
塑膠件製造商。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 100 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，100 
【大盤指數/股價】46128 / 82.6

➢ 2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業
費用為 9.5 億元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期
的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36 元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優
於預期。2Q26 營收 56.63 億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36
元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
➢ 3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%，使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現。
目前客戶之庫存水位約在一個月左右，而越靠近北半球之 AM 需求旺季
時，客戶有將庫存備至 2~3 個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，
因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升至 95%左右之水準 ， 同事帶動
毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長 。2026 上半年之 AM 件月營收約落在 14.4
億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收能提升
至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續
有望以此為低標向上成長 。OEM 方面 ， 台灣約佔32%， 中國則佔約68%，
台灣市場預計將微幅成長 3%，中國則估計衰退個位數百分比。 
總體而言，受惠 2026 下半年邁入傳統 AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季
成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元， QoQ+4.15%，
YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
➢ 股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長，公司持續開發的模具數量
也成為高度的競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進
行競爭。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，
YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29 元，YoY-6.53% / +4.65%。
當前評價約位於 12 倍，位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣位置，當前預
估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

個股報告

公司介紹 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主要營業項目為汽機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應商，也是全
球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，AM 塑膠件市占率約達 70%，而 AM 鈑金件全球
市佔率約 38%。除了在台南本廠有生產基地外，在桃園、中國大陸、美國、義大利等
地共設有 25 座生產據點。公司產品項目多元，包括引擎蓋、儀表板、車內飾板、行
李箱蓋、擾流板、前後保險桿、水箱護罩、水箱支架等塑料/鈑金件，產品多元使公司
可提供全球 85%~90%的碰撞零組件，約當超過 25,000 個品項，且每年持續投入約
18 億元之模具開發費用。產品營收佔比方面，AM 營收佔比約 75%、OEM 營收佔比
約 25%。以 AM 產品組合畫分來看，其中以保險桿為大宗，約佔 40%，水箱護罩、
水箱支架、葉子鈑、引擎蓋等分別佔 14%、3%、10%、11%。OEM 產品組合則以保
險桿為主，約佔 31%，儀錶板、水箱護罩、模具分別佔 17%、3%、7%。 
圖 1：東陽 AM 件產品項目

資料來源：東陽公司資料 
2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業費用為 9.5 億
元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36
元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優於預期。關稅在非半導體 232 關稅優惠
措施實施前，客戶拉貨仍面臨 27.5%之關稅，使庫存回補力道減弱，而 2Q 本來就為
該產業的營運淡季，庫存回補需求持續偏弱，導致 4 月、5 月、6 月營收持續呈現月
減之狀態 ， 預估2Q26 為全年營運谷底 ， 後續獲利將穩步向上回升 。2Q26 營收 56.63
億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36 元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%， 使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現 。 目前客戶之庫
存水位約在一個月左右， 而越靠近北半球之AM 需求旺季時 ，客戶有將庫存備至2~3
個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升
至 95%左右之水準，同事帶動毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長。2026 上半年之 AM 件
月營收約落在 14.4 億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收
能提升至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續有望以
此為低標向上成長。OEM 方面，台灣約佔 32%，中國則佔約 68%，台灣市場預計將

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
微幅成長 3%， 中國則估計衰退個位數百分比 。 總體而言 ， 受惠2026 下半年邁入傳統
AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元，
QoQ+4.15%，YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
長期 AM 件仍將逐漸轉往 OEM 件 使東陽可觸及市場穩步擴大 
展望未來，AM 市場持續在侵蝕 OEM 市場，以台灣而言，碰撞更換零組件市場中以
AM 為大宗，約佔 80%，OEM 則約佔 20%。美國使用 AM 件的佔比也從過去的個位
數%提升到目前的 21%，估計在 2030 年可達到 28%左右之水準。歐洲則從過去的
20+%提升到 30%。主因歐美地區的保險公司為了降低支出成本，持續鼓勵客戶選擇
使用品質與 OEM 件相近的 AM 件，一方面保險公司可以降低支出成本，另一方面被
保險人可以降低他的保費支出，達成雙贏的局面，也是AM 件在全球的佔比能持續穩
步提升的主要原因 。 因此在AM 件滲透率持續提升的狀況下 ， 公司每年的AM 件營收
都能保有 5%~8%的穩定成長，屬於穩健成長的經營型態。 
在競爭優勢方面，公司模具數量多，可提供超過 25,000 個品項，涵蓋了全球 80%在
路上跑的車型，且模具開發成本高、開發時間長(平均需約 6~8 個月)，東陽每年持續
開發 300~400 套模具，形成了高度的進入門檻。使AM 件的市佔率能維持在 70%以
上的高市佔率表現 。 除此之外 ， 保險公司理賠的AM 件還必須通過 CAPA 認證 ，CAPA
為美國獨立認證組織(Certified Automotive Parts Association) ，通過認證的零件表
示其品質與 OEM 件相同或是更高，在歐美市場更受到保險公司(如 State Farm)及消
費者的信任。 
股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長 ， 公司持續開發的模具數量也成為高度的
競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進行競爭。研究員預估 2026
年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29
元，YoY-6.53%/+4.65%。當前評價約位於 12 倍， 位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣
位置，當前預估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

豐興(2015) 
區間操作, TP 68 (潛在報酬 6.58%)  
區間操作 
Price(08/31)：63.8 
Target Price：68

豐興 26Q2 雖營收年減 9.20% 至 75.87 億元，但受惠型鋼與條
線出貨成長及產品組合優化，毛利率攀升至 12.74%；加計業外認
列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻 ， 稅後淨利達 8.47 億元 ，YoY 26.61%，
EPS 為 1.45 元。  展望 2026 全年，受鋼筋出貨承壓影響，預
估營收為 272.14 億元 ，YoY -6.28%； 但在型鋼與條線出貨維穩，
以及第二季業外收益的挹注下 ， 預估全年稅後淨利將成長至 25.31 
億元，YoY 12.28%，預估全年 EPS 為 4.35 元。   
評價方面 ， 按照過往 75% 配息率估算 ，2027 年有望配發 3.2 元
現金股利， 隱含殖利率約 5.1%。 考量獲利雖優於 2025 年且具備
殖利率支撐 ， 但未來整體營運動能受限 ， 故給予歷史本淨比中緣位
階 1.7 倍 PBR 評價，目標價設定為 68 元，投資建議為區間操
作。 
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
豐興鋼鐵(2015)成立於 1969 年，為台灣主要電爐鋼廠之一，
也是國內唯一同時具備型鋼 、 條線及鋼筋三大產品線的煉軋合
一鋼鐵廠。 
產品組合方面，鋼筋約佔營收 54%，市占率約 14.4%、條線
佔 25%、型鋼佔 21%。產能配置方面，豐興擁有85 噸 DC 電
弧爐和 100 噸 AC 電弧爐各一座 ， 搭配三條直列式軋鋼設備，
年產能達 180 萬噸。公司採用垂直整合的生產模式，具備完
整的煉鋼到軋鋼生產線，不僅可自給自足鋼胚，更能有效控制
生產成本。在原料採購策略上，豐興採取分批小量採購的方
式，有效降低原料價格波動風險。主要原料廢鐵和鋼胚約占成
本的六到七成，

⚫ 鋼筋銷售下滑，營收年減 9.20%至 75.87 億，EPS 1.45 元 
26Q2 鋼筋銷售年減 2 成，型鋼出貨與條線出貨年增，合併營
收 75.87 億，QoQ 18.47% ，YoY -9.20%；獲利受出貨季增
且產品組合優化 ， 毛利率升至 12.74%， 季增 1.20 個百分點，
年增 1.33 個百分點；營業利益 7.34 億，QoQ 24.47%，YoY 
-0.90%；業外認列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻，稅後獲利攀升至  
8.47 億，QoQ 60.83%，YoY 26.61%；EPS 為 1.45 元。

 ⚫ 型鋼與條線持穩，鋼筋貨動能保守，估全年 EPS 為 4.34 元

型鋼需求表現堅挺，預期全年出貨呈現持平至小幅成長，條線
目前雖處庫存消化階段，且下游需求呈分化 （螺帽與機械零組
件相對穩健，螺絲與手工具偏保守） ，但兩項產品展現出強韌
的定價能力；在原料跌價時報價依然穩定，價格與利差具備實
質支撐，預期將成為後市營收與獲利的核心動能。   
綜合評估，在鋼筋出貨承壓、型鋼與條線獲利護航的結構下，
預估全年營收為 272.14 億元，YoY -6.28%。受惠於產品組
合優化，預估毛利率將提升至  12.71%，年增 1.18 個百分
點，營業利益達 25.91 億元，YoY 3.54% 。加計第二季轉投
資等業外收益的顯著挹注，預估稅後淨利將攀升至 25.31 億
元，YoY 12.28%， 全年 EPS 為 4.35 元 ， 每股淨值約 39.91 
元。

筋出貨承壓影響，預估營收為 272.14 億元，YoY -6.28%；
但在型鋼與條線出貨維穩，以及第二季業外收益的挹注下，預

## Document 052: 2330台積電_1150831_BofA

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電_1150831_BofA.pdf

### Page 1

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co.

SEMICON Taiwan: proliferating AI boosting 
the manufacturing innovation

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 3,100 TWD | Price: 2,420 TWD

HPC computing drives holistic manufacturing innovation 
SEMICON Taiwan kicked off on August 31st with IC Forum and Silicon Photonics Summit, 
both featuring TSMC’s presentations. At the session in IC Forum, TSMC shared its view 
that the data center power capacity will accelerate from 5-6GW per year from ‘15-24 to 
30-40GW per year from ‘25-30, reaching 300GW installed base by ’30 as AI training 
scales toward multimodal (5x per annum LLM compute growth). In addition, it sees 
robust AI inference demand, with token consumption growing ~500x since ’22 led by 
agents and reasoning and further compounding by 24x to 120 quadrillion tokens by ‘30. 
TSMC has AI platform across logic (A13, A12, N2U). Si-technology for HBM scaling (from 
N12 for HBM4 to N3 for HBM5) and system integration (e.g. SoIC with 11 tape outs for 
GPU/CPU by 4Q26 and >14x reticle size CoWoS by ‘29) and compute fabric (e.g. CPO). 
Silicon photonics acceleration to enable faster I/O scaling 
TSMC believes the optical I/O is becoming prerequisite for AI/HPC system as the I/O gap 
is widening between the compute scaling (3x every 2 years) and I/O scaling (1.4x every 
two years). Its COUPE platform and a broad photonics device/PDK library facilitate its 
customer designs to enhance the connectivity through two complementary paths – 
“faster and narrowing” with higher per lane speed (200Gbps to 400Gbps) and lane count 
(128+ lanes) and “slower and wider” by increasing wavelengths from 1 to 16+ through 
WDM. To allow further production ramp and wider adoption of the technology platform, 
TSMC has lowered COUPE grating coupler insertion loss while improved edge coupler 
performance and sees wafer level testing is critical to reduce the wasted packaging cost. 
Stock at mid-cycle supported by robust AI demand pipeline 
We maintain our Buy rating with PO at NT$3,100 on 20x '27 P/E. The share valuation is 
undemanding at 16x '27 P/E on strong technology leadership and extending AI visibility.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,100 per share (US$590 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 18x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-40% 5-year CAGR 
in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

全新 (2455 TT)  Buy

2H26 、 2027 年營收動能可期，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：07/2026 全新營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史
新高，主要係 AI data center 相關需求持續熱絡，且出貨表現亦
隨磷化銦基板料況改善而回歸常軌所致，且 2H26、2027 年營收
動能仍可期，故群益對全新的投資建議為 Buy 。  
07/2026 營收創新高： 2Q26 營收 9.65 億元， QoQ+0.63% ，其中，
微電子營收 7.04 億元， QoQ-1.89% ，與 1Q26 相去有限；光電子
產品營收 2.61 億元， QoQ+8.12% ，但 01~06/2026 僅 02/2026 受
農曆春節影響而僅 0.68 億元，其餘各月份光電子營收均為
0.86~0.88 億元，換言之，缺料問題仍影響光電子產品出貨表現。  
不過， 07/2026 營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史新高，關
鍵在於磷化銦基板缺料狀況將隨中國政府 05/2026 、 07/2026 兩
度放行管制而改善 ， 帶動全新光電子產品營收增加至 1.69 億元 ，
MoM+93.74% ；至於微電子產品營收 2.53 億元，亦為近幾年相
對高水準。  
2H26 展望優於 1H26：雖然 AI data center 相關需求熱絡，然而，
01~06/2026 光電子產品月營收成長性仍受制於磷化銦基板缺
料。全新磷化銦基板過去多數由美國業者的中國廠供應，然而，
受到中國政府管制磷化銦基板出口數量影響， 4Q25 全新光電子
產品營收逐月減少，且全新來自該供應商的磷化銦基板庫存
12/2025 已陸續用罄， 01~06/2026 磷化銦基板主要由第 2、 3 供
應商供應。  
所幸，中國政府 05/2026 月底批准 2026 年第 1 批磷化銦基板出
口， 07/2026 則再通過第 2 批，且總數量大於第 1 批，第 1、 2
批合計數量更已超過 2025 年總數量，此外，全新 06/2026 已陸
續接獲第 1 批獲准出口的磷化銦基板並用以生產光電子產品，
2H26 展望優於 1H26 。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量目標而言，由於全新專注於製造、銷售及研發化
合物半導體磊晶片，溫室氣體排放量多寡，主要和能源耗用、產能及產品組
成有關。全新乃就單位產品的能源耗用與溫室氣體排放量，設定目標為每年
減少 5%，並將視產能擴充與產品變化情形，賡續追蹤與調整設定與目標。
至於企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果的部份，全新為客戶代
工生產的產品多數運用於通訊等電子產品，非屬終端消費性產品；全新除協
助客戶設計低耗能、高效率產品，以降低產品生命週期內的資源耗用外，亦
持續進行生產製造過程的節能與減廢，以降低對環境所造成的衝擊，減少單
位產品所消耗的資源與污染物，為客戶及社會創造減碳環保效益。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

MPI
Valuation
Our target price for MPI is set at NT$7,200, based on a target PE multiple of 52x applied to our 2027-28E EPS average. We
believe our PE target, at the high end of MPI's 3-year forward PE range, is justified given MPI’s earnings power remains
structurally higher than that in the previous cycles, broadening demand across AI ASIC, additional CPU opportunities and non-
AI ASIC wins. We expect MPI to see accelerating earnings growth ahead supported by sustainable sales growth and an

improving mix. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027E/28E P/B of 23x/15x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the MPI stock as High Risk for its share-price volatility. Key downside risks that could
prevent MPI shares from reaching our target price include 1) slowdown in AI ASIC demand; 2) slower-than-expected adoption
of MEMS probe cards; 3) intensifying competition from global and regional peers; 4) slow development of the CPO equipment

business.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

2Q26 beat: Inventec’s 2Q26 revenue was 10% / 14% higher than our estimate / 
Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the AI server shipments ramp up and PC 
demand pull-in. GM of 4.2% was lower than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, 
reflecting the rising component cost weighing on the company’s profitability, such as 
PCB, memory, CPU, etc. 2Q26 opex ratio was better than our estimate and Bloomberg 
consensus, reflecting the company’s improving operational efficiency as revenue scales 
up, along with the higher than expected non-op income, leading 2Q26 net income 27% 
higher than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Inventec’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and slightly cut 
our 2026-28E net income by 5% / 6% / 1%, mainly on lower GM estimates. Our 
2026-28E revenues are revised up to reflect the company’s strong AI server business 
growth, riding on the growing AI infrastructure trend globally and enjoying a product 
mix upgrade towards AI servers with stronger performance and higher ASP . We cut our 
2026-28E GMs to reflect the 2Q26 GM miss and the impact of rising component costs. 
2026-28E opex ratios are revised down, mainly on higher operational efficiency as AI 
server shipments grow. We raise our interest expenses in 2026-28E to reflect Inventec’s 
debt level increase, supporting the company’s global capacity expansion and rising 
working capital level.

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP , and our target P/E is 
updated to 14.6x 2027E P/E (vs. 13.6x previously), mainly on higher earnings growth in 
2027-28E, still derived from PC/server peers’ correlation between forward year trading 
P/E and earnings growth. With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, our 12M

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$56.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.6x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 068: 260828_ml_unimicron

Source PDF: all report pdf/260828_ml_unimicron.pdf

### Page 1

Unimicron

Limited clarity on the investigation; no 
material impact so far

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,400 TWD | Price: 1,110 TWD

Breaking News 
• According to the company’s announcement, Unimicron is being investigated by 
Taoyuan Investigation Bureau. Our supply chain checks and conversation with the 
company suggest this is on a matter relating to the labeling of products' origin. 
• Our supply chain checks and conversation with the company suggest that this does 
not appear to be related to accounting fraud or insider trading, as has been reported 
in some news items in the press. 
• Based on the limited information we have currently as described above, we do not see 
a major impact on the company's business/operation at this stage.  However, we do 
anticipate that uncertainty is likely to continue until there is further clarification.

Unimicron under investigation; share price dropped 
According to the company’s announcement, Taoyuan Investigation Bureau visited the 
company today to conduct a search/investigation, primarily to examine matters relating 
to “Offenses Against Agriculture, Industry, and Commerce”. The share price of 
Unimicron went down 5.9% today, vs 4.3% and 1.6% falls in the other two major 
substrate names NYPCB (Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board) and Kinsus.  
More granularities on the exact matter 
Our checks with the supply chain and with the company suggest this matter could be 
related to potential uncertainties associated with the labeling of products’ origin. 
Further, it appears it is neither related to accounting fraud nor insider trading, as has 
been reported in some news articles.  
Not seeing major impact on the business so far 
Currently we believe that in this case, based on the  information we have at this stage, 
there would only be a minor impact (if any) to the company’s product shipments, 
echoing the company’s statement of “no material impact on financial condition or 
business operation”. For other substrate names, we haven’t heard relevant repercussions 
so far.

### Page 2

Price objective basis & risk 
Unimicron (UMCRF) 
Our PO of NT$1,400 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E, which is roughly 1x standard 
deviation above the historical average of 20x since 2017. In our view, the multiple is 
appropriate as we expect Unimicron earnings to be supported by its high-end technology 
offerings, where end market demand seems more resilient.

Upside risks are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) greater PC and regular server demand, and (3) 
lower competition pressure.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) lower ASP content increase 
at AI server, and (3) bigger drop in the demand of consumer electronic products.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on final JMTBA orders, which we believe
could be among the catalysts for Airtac's and Hiwin's share
prices.
Per the Japan Machine Tool Builders' Association (JMTBA), headline orders
dropped 5% MoM but grew 50% YoY in July 2026 (vs. +15% MoM/+53% YoY in
June 2026), to ¥193bn.
• Domestic orders  -8% MoM/+50% YoY (vs.+28% MoM/+46% YoY    in June), to
¥53bn
• Overseas orders  -4% MoM/+51% YoY (vs. +10% MoM/+56% YoY  in June), to

¥140bn
• By region:
° China: -14% MoM/+52% YoY, to ¥49bn
° Europe: +20% MoM/+41% YoY, to ¥23bn
° North America: -1% MoM/+51% YoY, to ¥40bn
Our view:
• For overseas orders, we continue to see solid growth on a YoY basis pointing
to a continuous upcycle, while sequential fluctuations are largely consistent
with seasonality in major regions.
• On top of overall segment recovery within automation:
° Airtac (1590.TW, OW) should benefit from its own share gains and

incremental contributions from linear motion components. Valuation
appears undemanding at 21x 2027e P/E (vs. 25x average since 2020).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show margin expansion in the coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside as the stock now trades at 30x 2027e P/E
(vs. peak cycle valuation of 35-40x).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

•MediaTek becomes a much more credible AI ASIC contender . MediaTek 
already expects its first major AI accelerator ASIC to enter production in 
4Q26, has guided to >$2bn of data-center revenue in 2026, estimates a 
roughly US$80bn 2027 addressable/serviceable market, and has raised its 
target share to 15–20%.   The NVIDIA relationship therefore potentially 
expands MediaTek’s opportunity beyond its existing hyperscaler programs: 
NVLink Fusion can become a customer-acquisition platform for future 
second customer's ASIC wins as NVLink Fusion changes the competitive 
proposition: instead of MediaTek selling customers only an independently 
designed ASIC, it can potentially offer a custom XPU already engineered to 
coexist with NVIDIA GPUs, CPUs, networking and rack-scale NVLink 
infrastructure. According to NVIDIA, the cooperation will be deepening 
including : NVIDIA NVLink Fusion chiplet, connecting XPUs to the NVIDIA 
NVLink scale-up fabric using NVIDIA photonics or electrical 
interconnects;  NVIDIA NVLink-C2C, providing high-bandwidth, energy-
efficient connectivity between XPUs, NVIDIA Rosa CPUs and other 
compatible processors. NVIDIA NVHBM, integrating customized memory 
capabilities to increase bandwidth and energy efficiency while dedicating 
more silicon area to compute.
•MediaTek is evolving from a smartphone SoC company into an AI 
compute-platform . MediaTek and NVIDIA announced the GB10 platform 
last year, where MediaTek contributed Arm CPU/SoC and power-efficiency 
expertise to the Grace Blackwell-based DGX Spark platform.   That 
relationship has already expanded into RTX Spark for Windows AI PCs, and 
today’s announcement explicitly says the two companies intend to

collaborate across multiple generations of both RTX Spark and DGX Spark products.   MediaTek therefore gains three
separate AI growth vectors: (1) hyperscaler custom AI ASICs, (2) NVIDIA-based AI PCs/local AI systems, and (3)
automotive/physical AI. Strategically, this is important because MediaTek can reuse the same core capabilities—
leading-edge CPU/SoC design, SerDes/interconnect, advanced packaging, connectivity and power optimization—

across all three markets. The result should be a progressively higher mix of large, advanced-node chips with much
greater dollar content than traditional smartphone SoCs, reducing MediaTek’s dependence on handset cycles.
•We view today’s announcement as structurally positive for MediaTek. The US$3.5bn investment validates the
partnership financially, it potentially moves MediaTek from being a supplier that happens to have a few large AI ASIC
wins toward becoming one of the principal custom-silicon design platforms inside the broader NVIDIA AI-factory

ecosystem. That could materially increase both the number and longevity of MediaTek’s AI ASIC opportunities in the
longer term.

### Page 3

Mediatek (2454.TW)
31 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 074: 260831_daiwa_ Kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260831_daiwa_ Kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-31
Pages: 5

### Page 1

Summary: We hosted an online investor conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 
31 August. We believe that management’s current revenue target is 15% QoQ growth in 
3Q26 (vs. market expectation of 10% QoQ). Regarding the investigation into Unimicron’s 
original place of production, the company noted that it has not seen any order shift from 
end customers. However, it believes this event will not be a negative factor for other 
substrate makers. In addition, Kinsus’ 9% share price decline today has nothing to do with 
the investigation into Unimicron. Rather, it is more likely related to its parent company’s 
plan to sell 16 million shares in Kinsus (announced last Friday). Kinsus believes this was 
driven by financial management considerations, and not by any specific intention to reduce 
its stake in Kinsus. If Kinsus’ share price continues to rise, management does not rule out 
the possibility that its parent company may trim its stake again. Overall, we believe Kinsus’ 
fundamentals remain largely in line with our expectations, with raw material prices 
continuing to rise and substrate supply tightening gradually. We maintain our Buy (1) rating 
on the stock, with a 12-month target price of TWD1,068, based on a 36x PER on the 1-
year forward EPS. Key downside risks include weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs, and AI servers.

We hosted an online conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 31 August. Overall, 
we believe the business outlook to be largely on track with our previous note (link here, 
published on 31 July). Below are our key takeaways from the conference.

Recent updates:  
• We believe the company has revised up its 3Q26 revenue growth target from 10% QoQ 
to 15% QoQ, reflecting a better-than-expected benefit from price increases. Looking 
ahead to 4Q26, management still expects growth of 10% QoQ, in line with its previous 
comments. 
• Revenue mix in 2Q26: ABF 45%, BT 38%, and contact lens 17%. Looking ahead, the 
company expects the revenue mix from ABF to gradually increase to 50–55% in 2027.

Price hike & margin expansion: 
• The company maintains its target of a 7-8% ASP increase per quarter in 2H26 to reflect 
rising costs of raw materials, including glass fibre, copper foil, drill bits, and ABF build-up 
film. 
• Although some competitors have highlighted a much larger magnitude of price hikes, 
Kinsus has not seen a similar level of increase. The company also believes the price 
hike level for ABF will be higher than that for BT in 2H26, whereas in 1H26 the price hike 
for BT was higher than that for ABF. 
• Looking into 2H26 and 2027, the company expects raw material ASPs to continue rising. 
As such, single-digit price hikes should be possible, with raw materials currently 
accounting for 30% of BT’s COGS. In addition, the driver of ASP increases is expected 
to gradually shift from raw material inflation to substrate capacity shortages.  
• In terms of gross margin expansion, Kinsus believes this will be driven mainly by an 
improved product mix and rising utilisation rates, rather than price hikes. This is similar 
to Unimicron. However, NYPCB’s gross margin expansion could be driven by internal 
price hikes within the Nanya Group.

Country

31 August 2026

MEMO 
 Key takeaways from investor conference call

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT)
Share price (31 Aug): TWD819.00 12-mth rating: Buy (1) Information Technology: Taiwan
Sheng Cheng

### Page 2

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT) 
31 August 2026 
2

Utilisation rates (UTR): 
• ABF: The company expects the UTR to increase from 85% (beginning of 2026) to 95% 
(at the end of 2026). 
• BT: The company expects the UTR to increase from 75% (beginning of 2026) to 85% (at 
the end of 2026).

Capacity expansion plan 
• Given capacity constraints, Kinsus will gradually expand its ABF capacity. It currently 
has around a 40 million ABF substrate capacity per month and plans to increase this to 
80 million by 2029. 
• According to management, the major equipment pull-in cycle will occur in 2027. After 
that, most equipment will be subsidised by end customers, and the depreciation 
expense should decline to TWD6.5-7.0bn from 2028 onward, versus TWD7.1-7.2bn in 
2026 and 2027. 
• As depreciation currently accounts for 15% of COGS, Kinsus believes it will reduce its 
selling prices slightly after clients begin subsidising the equipment, as the benefit will be 
shared between clients and Kinsus, at 7-8% each.

New growth opportunities – ABF 
• We believe Kinsus will secure a higher allocation for both CPU and GPU products from 
a global leading GPU brand in its next-generation products, given the current industry’s 
supply constraints. 
• The company also believes it will provide additional support to other GPU brands for 
their GPU products from 2027. 
• Outside of GPUs, the company sees less progress in the ASIC segment for now 
compared with GPUs. It has secured around a 10% allocation for one major ASIC 
product, but progress in other ASIC products remains relatively limited.

New growth opportunities – BT  
• The company believes pluggable optical modules and market share gains (as 
competitors gradually exit the BT market) will be key growth drivers for Kinsus. 
• According to management, the BT substrate used in optical modules will have 14 layers, 
compared with the mainstream 4-layer format today. Its existing equipment can support 
both 4-layer and 14-layer BT substrates. However, the company may need to add some 
routing capacity to support optical module demand.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

## Document 075: 260831_mq_MTK NV ECB

Source PDF: all report pdf/260831_mq_MTK NV ECB.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-31
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Flashnote
31 August 2026
MediaTek
NVDA and GOOG subscribe to MTK's US$3.9bn
ECB
What's new
• Today, MediaTek announced the largest ECB ever in the Taiwan market.
MediaTek announced today that it is issuing a US$3.9bn (>NT$120bn)
overseas convertible bond (ECB), the largest ECB ever from a Taiwan issuer
(see news here). NVIDIA subscribed US$3.5bn (~90%), which marks its
first major direct investment in a Taiwan-listed company. Alphabet also
subscribed (amount undisclosed). Per the announcement, the conversion
price is set at ~NT$4,500 (i.e. 115% of its 31 Aug close). Proceeds fund
foreign-currency material procurement, and the company disclosed a max
dilution of ~1.67%.
• NVIDIA and Alphabet subscribed. In a subsequent interview, NVIDIA and
MediaTek announced a deepened partnership across 1) AI infrastructure:
MediaTek adopts the NVLink Fusion platform, giving hyperscalers, CSPs
and frontier labs a prebuilt, pre-validated path to bring custom XPUs into
NVLink-connected rack-scale AI factories, with NVIDIA networking and CPUs
pulled through when MediaTek wins; 2) local AI computing: multi-generation
DGX Spark/RTX Spark PC roadmap; and 3) automotive: Dimensity Auto
with DRIVE AGX. MediaTek mgmt reiterated AI chip revenue of ~US$2bn
this year and its target of ~15% of an ~US$80bn TAM next year, which it
expects NVLink Fusion to support by shortening customers’ time to market.
Why it matters
• Endorsement of MediaTek's AI strategy. NVIDIA mgmt commented that
the investment formalises a partnership built on a decade-long roadmap,
and it is NVIDIA's first major investment in a Taiwan-listed company. We
believe a commitment of this scale is hard to repeat regularly, positioning
MediaTek as NVIDIA's partner of choice in the custom-XPU expansion,
marking an endorsement that peers are unlikely to obtain and confirmation
of MediaTek's expansion from AI ASIC chips into full rack-scale systems and
platforms.
• Dilution is small, and overhang risk is low. Disclosed max dilution is only
~1.67%. Moreover, >90% of the deal sits with strategic subscribers (i.e.
NVIDIA, Alphabet), who we believe will not run the delta-hedging (short-
selling) that typically pressure shares after an ECB issuance.
• Partnership enlarges MediaTek's ASIC opportunity. Through NVLink
Fusion, every MediaTek XPU rack pulls through NVIDIA switching, networking
& CPUs, so NVIDIA gains from MediaTek's ASIC success. We believe NVIDIA
chose MediaTek because it is open to both GPU-attached & ASIC business,
whereas peers compete with NVIDIA in networking or are less aligned. This
supports mgmt's target of ~15% of an US$80bn TAM next year.
What now
• Reiterate Outperform, TP at NT$10,000.
Semicons & Semicon Equip
Taiwan

M
Update
Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Credo's
FY1Q27 Results
We see positive implications for Bizlink from Credo's comments
on expanding AEC   and emerging ALC opportunities.
FY1Q27 (ended July 2026) results:
• Credo (CRDO.US, not covered) reported revenue of US$479mn (+10% QoQ/
+115% YoY), roughly in line with the consensus of US$474mn and guidance
of US$465-475mn.
• Non-GAAP GM was 68% (-0.3ppt QoQ) in line with consensus at 68% and

guidance at 67-69%.
• Non-GAAP NP was US$236mn (+4% QoQ/+140% YoY), roughly in line with
consensus at US$232mn.
• Four customers each contributed more than 10% of Credo's revenue in
FY1Q27, i.e., 33%, 28%, 13% and 10%, respectively.
FY2Q27 and FY27 outlook:
•  FY2Q27 revenue is guided to be US$525-535mn (+11% QoQ/+98% YoY at the
midpoint, largely in line with consensus of US$526mn), non-GAAP GM of 67-
69% (vs. consensus at 67.5%) and opex of US$100-105mn.

• For FY27, Credo now expects total revenue to grow >85% YoY (vs.
consensus at ~86% YoY), with an  inflection in 2H mainly from optical
revenue (>US$600mn for FY27 from Zero-Flap Optics, silicon photonics PICs
and optical DSPs, with each to contribute >US$100mn).
• Credo expects its FY27 GM to stay flat YoY, while operating expenses might
increase by ~55% YoY.
• 1.6T AEC might start to emerge from 2HFY27.
• ALCs and OmniConnect gearboxes remain on track for initial revenue from

FY28, management believes both are multi-billion dollar opportunities for
Credo.
• It also sees increasing customer activity on NPO for scale up network, with
confirmed PIC design wins for ramp in FY28.
Bizlink Read-across:
• We see positive implications from Credo's comments on the AEC's spec
upgrade and its penetration into more hyperscalers and neoclouds, as well as
the reiterated timing of ALC ramp in FY28.
• We stay constructive on Bizlink for its multiple drivers in the AI deployment

Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We think our target P/E multiple is justified by an
earnings CAGR of 46% seen over 2025-28e, which implies a PEG of around 0.7. This is in line
with the average of 0.7 in 2019-24, when the stock was trading at an average 13x P/E for a 19%

business

Risks to Downside
n Lower-than-expected AEC adoption
n Intensifying competition
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower-than-expected growth in the semi capital equipment market
n Continued weakness in the auto industry

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

### Page 4

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

Quanta’s Jul revenue was down 5% QoQ / up 131% YoY, or 21% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, along with the 
stronger than expected PC shipments at 4.5m units. Looking ahead, we expect 
sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC 
end demand. We model sequential revenue increases in Oct-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
11x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 6

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): July revenues sequentially increase; Capex strong increase shows strong AI 
demand

Wiwynn July revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$118bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI servers 
(air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we model 
sequential revenues decline in Aug and Sep, considering the high-base of general 
servers, while liquid-cooled ASIC AI servers of its major customer, and GPU AI server 
racks ramp up is in 4Q26E. Nevertheless, the 3Q26 revenues growth would be much 
stronger than 1Q26 (-5% QoQ), and 2Q26 (+1% QoQ), up to +22% QoQ, and we expect

servers expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU expansion, and less 
impact of business model transition from Buy & Sell to Consign for general servers. 
Maintain Buy.

based on 20x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

### Page 8

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

Gigabyte (2376.TW, Neutral): AI server in expansion; Strong capex commitment to capture the

Gigabyte’s Jul revenue was up 60% YoY / 3% MoM, or 18% higher than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the strong shipments performance of both baseboard and 
rack-level AI servers. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Sep, supported by the 
rising AI server shipments, and we expect revenues to stay high in 3Q / 4Q26E post 
strong growth in 1H26, driving 2H26 revenues growth at +15% HoH. Management 
remains positive on 2H26 growth driven by strong AI demand, customers’ faster AI 
deployment, and Gigabyte’s expanding customer base and enhancing services. 
Management expects their customers’ infrastructure readiness and components 
procurement to continue to improve in 2H26, supporting the procurement on AI servers. 
Maintain Neutral.

based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

### Page 10

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): PC enjoying product mix upgrade; AI server client base expansion; July & 
2Q26 beat

ASUS’s Jul revenue was up 53% YoY / down 21% QoQ to NT$84bn, or 16% higher than 
our estimate, reflecting strong AI servers demand, along with PC product mix upgrade. 
Looking forward, we expect sequential revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low 
base in Jul, followed by sequential revenue declines in Oct-Nov, as the rising memory 
cost would weigh PC end demand and AI servers model transition. Overall, our estimate 
shows double-digit YoY revenue growth for 3Q / 4Q26E, supported by PC product mix 
upgrade and rack-level AI server ramp up. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,109. Our target 
price is based on 15.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

### Page 11

Compal (2324.TW, Neutral): AI server capacity in expansion; July better than expected

Compal’s Jul revenue was up 38% YoY / down 15% MoM to NT$81bn, or 10% higher 
than our estimate, reflecting AI servers shipment ramp up. Jul PC shipments was down 
22% YoY / 25% MoM to 1.8m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid 
the rising memory cost. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, and PC product 
mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect Compal’s 3Q / 
4Q26E revenues to grow at 6% / 10% QoQ, benefiting from the rising AI server end 
demand from Neocloud clients. We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, 
supported by global capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, and Vietnam, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, which demand 
could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on 
fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$41.3. Our target 
price is based on 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

### Page 14

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server driving 2H26 growth; PC growth capped by rising memory cost; Sell

Pegatron’s Jul revenue was +12% YoY / -6% MoM to NT$86bn, and we attribute the 
MoM decline to the sluggish PC end demand post the demand pull-in in 1H26, causing 
Pegatron’s PC shipments to decline to 0.40m units in Jul from 0.65m units in Jun. We 
model double digit MoM revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low base in Jul, 
followed by modest sequential growth in Oct to Dec, mainly driven by the strong AI 
server shipments growth. Management remains positive on the momentum of AI server 
business in 2H26E, supported by the strong end demand from Neocloud and CSP clients. 
Overall, we expect Pegatron’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at +20% / +27% YoY. We are 
positive on Pegatron’s AI server business expansion, while we expect the tight memory 
supply to cap the consumer electronics growth. Maintain Sell.

Earnings revision: Pegatron’s 2Q26 revenue, GM, and Opex ratio are largely in line with 
our estimate, while 2Q26 non-op income is higher than expected, leading 2Q26 net 
income 22% higher than our estimate. We factor in Pegatron’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 
result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes by 10% / 8% / 7%, mainly on higher revenues 
and lower opex ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 5% / 5% / 4%, mainly on 
higher contribution from AI server business. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are slightly 
revised up, mainly on higher R&D expense to support the AI server specification 
upgrades and PC product mix upgrade towards AI PC.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

穩懋 (3105 TT)  Buy

PIN 等通訊新訂單出貨量將持續放大，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：由於 PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產，且
預估出貨量 3Q26 將持續放大，後續成長空間可期，尤其，根據
美國媒體路透報導指出，美國政府現正起草一項禁止中國新型
光纖收發器出貨至美國的禁令，以保護當地 AI 基礎設施，由於
穩懋 PIN 等光通訊訂單多數來自美國客戶，預估將為受惠者之
一，故群益對穩懋的投資建議為 Buy 。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 QoQ+83.22% ： 2Q26 營收 52.57 億元，
QoQ+14.54% ，動能來自 cellular 、infrastructure 、optical 等產品，
此外，雖然 optical 新產品量產初期良率較低，不過，由於 2Q26
產品結構優於 1Q26 、產能利用率提高，毛利率提高至 28.24% ，
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+72.14% ，加上認列 1.44 億元匯兌利益、
2.70 億元投資利益， 2Q26 稅後淨利增加至 9.77 億元，
QoQ+83.22% 。  
就 2Q26 的產品結構而言， cellular 產品營收 QoQ+18.00% ，原
因在於手機業者需求隨逐漸進入旺季而增溫； infrastructure 產
品營收 QoQ+14.53% ，動能來自 AI data center 、低軌道衛星；  
optical 產品 2Q26 重要動能有二， (1) 新款 iPhone 相關 VCSEL
訂單數量增加， (2)PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產。  
3Q26 營收動能來自 optical 、infrastructure ：展望 3Q26，optical
產品受惠於 PIN 等光通訊新訂單出貨量放大，營收成長性居各
產品之冠，預估將帶動 others 產品營收 QoQ+35.97% ，且後續成
長空間仍可期；其次， infrastructure 產品出貨動能來自低軌道
衛星標案陸續出貨，預估相關營收 QoQ+20.45% ，由於全球各地
低軌道衛星標案隨應用領域拓展而有機會持續釋出，後續營收
前景亦仍佳。  
至於 cellular 、 Wi-Fi 產品出貨成長性雖受到近期記憶體缺料等
因影響整體手機產業景氣而存在不確定性，但現階段預估營收
均與 2Q26 實績相去有限。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，穩懋持續透過各項節能措
施提升廠內能源使用效率，以減少用電度數，且新設產線全面採用最佳可行
技術，實際措施包括 PFCs 尾氣處理設備全面採用去除效率達 90% 以上之型
式 (IPCC 公告指定型式 )、逐步汰換既設產線之 PFCs 尾氣處理設備、評估製
程氣體減量使用或取代高潛勢溫室氣體使用、採取工程改善或設備更換以
降低用電量、評估新建廠房設置廠內再生能源等。  
再就企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果而言，穩懋致力於新技
術的開發，持續推出高效能的技術平台，以生產更具能源效益和節能的半導
體通訊產業產品，因終端產品應用逐漸擴及手機、無線網路、雷達、衛星等
各個通訊領域，有助於全球能源節省和減少用電消耗。此外，藉由穩懋多功
能的晶片整合、製程線寬的縮減以及製程能力的提升，終端產品之尺寸、單
位晶片使用量均持續減少，亦得以為守護地球環境盡一份心力。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/張書明 brady.chang@uni-psg.com

研究部維持群創中立投資建議，係因：（1）1H26 已提前備貨，加上記
憶體等原物料影響，2H26 顯示業務仍具不確定性； （2） FOPLP 數倍成
長，然營收佔比仍僅低個位數； （3） 中長期玻璃基板新應用可期，然短
期貢獻有限。綜上所述，預估 26 年營收 2806 億元下修至 2557 億元
（+13%YoY），假設 4Q26 有望先認列南科 Fab 5 處分利益，預估 26 
年稅後 EPS 從 0.58 元上修至 2.33 元 ， 其中處分利益貢獻 1.23 元。
群創近年積極轉型，新應用題材多元發展，脫離過去面板紅海市場，使
得評價持續上修。研究部認爲新應用短期貢獻有限，26 年面板業務佔
比仍過半，面對面板產業的不確定性，目前評價合理，評等維持中立。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26 產品組合轉佳帶動毛利率大幅成長 
群創 2Q26 營收 637 億元（-4%QoQ，+13%YoY），符合預期，毛
利率 14.6％ （+0.16ppts QoQ，+6.22ppts YoY），產品組合為
消費性 45%、商用 13%、非顯示 42%，毛利率區間中值分別為
7.5%、17.5%、13%。非顯示營收佔比從 25 年同期的 28%，一路
提升至 42%，產品組合轉佳帶動營收與毛利成長。公司持續進行
成本控制及資產瘦身，營業費用從 81 億元季減至 76 億元。
2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.57 元，其中 0.37 元來自過去虧損扣抵所認
列的遞延所得稅利益貢獻。

2. 併購 Pioneer 帶動非顯示佔比顯著提升 
群創近年積極切入多項非顯示高利基應用 ， 跨及車用 、 智慧醫療 、
衛星通訊 、 半導體封裝 。6M25 子公司 CarUX 收購日商 Pioneer，
已於 12M25 完成交割並開始併表 ， 帶動月營收與獲利顯著提升 。
集團現提供完整智慧座艙解決方案，預期未來持續專注在整併、
研發 、 製造 ， 短期可能導致費用提升 ， 長期則可期綜效逐漸顯現 ，
成為 26 年主要成長動能。預期併表後車用營收翻倍成長，產品
組合轉佳帶動毛利提升。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 積極轉型切入先進封裝，26 年 FOPLP 數倍成長 
公司長期仍面臨顯示產業的挑戰，近年積極轉型，目前先以 5.5 
代以下的舊世代小尺寸面板廠轉型，過去曾出售廠房給晶圓廠、
封測廠等高科技產業，同時 25 年更透過南科 3.5 代廠轉型切
入 FOPLP，主要客戶為國際知名大廠，應用於 PMIC、RF、通訊、
車用感測等成熟應用 ， 取代既有 FOWLP、WLCSP 及部分傳統封裝 。
目前 FOPLP 月產量已從  25 年 400 萬顆晶片成長至超過  
4,000 萬顆，客戶接受度高且產品供不應求，2H26 有望持續成
長，預期 26 年貢獻數十億元。 
儘管如此，初期 FOPLP 實際投產供應商包括群創、力成、Nepess
年產能均達數億顆，長期低階應用還是可能面臨供過於求問題，
因此各家廠商將 FOPLP 視為技術過渡，持續朝高階應用發展。

圖一、 PLP 成本有望減少逾三成

資料來源：群創、統一投顧整理

4. 中長期與台積電、IBIDEN 三方合作開發玻璃基板 
隨著 AI 晶片尺寸變大，載板中有機層翹曲程度將大幅增加，良
率下降造成製程成本大幅增加。為因應 28-30 年 14x reticle 
size 晶片，供應鏈積極開發替代材料。6M26 台積電於 JPCA 首
度公開宣布與日本 ABF 載板龍頭 IBIDEN 與國內面板廠群創共
同開發玻璃核心基板 取代載版中 BT 有機層 。測試導入玻璃後  
Coplanarity 改善 16%、有效CTE 下降 19%、有效 Modulus 提
升 31%， 同時電阻下降 27%、 電感下降 42%， 顯示玻璃在大型 AI 
封裝的翹曲、機械穩定性及 Power Integrity 均具明顯優勢。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

圖二、 玻璃基板 GCS 結構

資料來源：台股產業研究室、IC Components、統一投顧整理

5. 供應鏈仍有多項挑戰，輕化資產與減資勢在必行 
供應鏈預期 2H27 進一步驗證 510×515mm 規格，部分玻璃切割
及 TGV 相關製程有機會利用群創長期累積的大尺寸玻璃加工能
力。群創此次法說也首度公開討論玻璃封裝應用，預期群創將利
用 Fab3 G5 廠房開發，該廠房產能約 11.5 萬片/月，G5 母片
玻璃尺寸 1,100×1,300mm，可提供 4 片 510*515mm GCS，每片 
GCS 則可放上 16 顆 14 reticle size (110x110mm) 晶片。假
設 G5 母片 11.5 萬片月產能滿載 ， 預期放量後將貢獻稅後 EPS 
2-4 元。 
假設 28 年多數 AI 晶片為 14× reticle size （110×110mm） ，單
片 510×515mm GCS 可供應 16 顆晶片。依目前晶片需求量，預期
GCS 年需求量約 90-100 萬片 。相較之下 ， 面板廠既有母玻璃產能
極大，單廠母片玻璃月產能多逾 10 萬片，潛在 GCS 年產能數百
萬片，當技術與產線成熟時，長期供給擴張速度可能非常快，仍
需考量供需平衡避免重蹈覆徹。 
儘管中長期供需維持健康水準 ，依目前晶片需求量對於面板廠龐
大的營收規模與股本而言，貢獻稅後EPS 仍僅低個位數。欲擺脫
面板產業的紅海市場，勢必需改變過往規模經濟的面板商業模
式，積極輕化資產與減資方能凸顯轉型效益。 
此外，短中期玻璃封裝供應鏈仍有多項挑戰。儘管 GCS 多數製程
與面板業、封裝業重疊，目前 TGV（玻璃穿孔）仍為製程最大瓶
頸，包括成孔、檢測、鍍銅等。時程方面，儘管公司於法說提到
最晚於 28 年落地，載板廠與設備廠仍認為成熟時機將於  30 
年，28-30 年仍將與有機層一同測試競爭，供應鏈、時程與貢獻
仍有變數，未來持續追蹤設備商等供應鏈進度。

6. 26 年新應用題材利多，然短期貢獻有限 
本業方面，1H26 因記憶體漲價缺料與冬奧、世足等運動賽事帶
動 TV、Monitor 需求 ， 然而 NB 與 IT 則受記憶體影響而衰退。
研究部認為 1H26 提前拉貨 ，2H26 拉貨動能勢必減緩 ，NB 與 IT 
將最受影響，保守預期 26 年消費顯示業務恐面臨雙位數衰退。
26 年成長動能主要來自車用，12M25 Poineer 併表後，可使車
用 26 年全年營收增加 500 億，同時切入日系車廠成爲智慧座
艙 Tier-1 廠商，綜效可期。近期公司新應用題材多元，然短期
貢獻仍有限。預期 26 年 FOPLP 貢獻數十億元，營收佔比仍僅
低個位數。中長期玻璃應用佈局仍需持續追蹤。綜上所述，預估 
26 年營收 2806 億元下修至 2557 億元 （+13%YoY） ， 假設 4Q26 
有望先認列南科 Fab 5 處分利益 ， 預估26 年稅後 EPS 從 0.58 
元上修至 2.33 元，其中處分利益貢獻 1.23 元。

400G ZR/ZR+ 目前維持穩定出貨，雷射晶片後段產能亦配合客戶
需求持續擴增；隨 800G ZR/ZR+ 預計於 2026H2 開始放量，公司
後續成長動能可望進一步提升。 產能方面， 華星光無塵室面積預計
於 2026 年成長 24%，2027 年再增加 67%，新增產能將同步支應
雷射產品及光收發模組代工需求。綜合以上，研究部預估華星光
2026 年 EPS 7.95 元，2027 年 EPS 16.99 元，目前 PE 為 36x，
參考國內光通訊類股 PE 區間為 20-50x，考量下一世代 800G 
ZR/ZR+產品將開始加入貢獻，另外雷射後段產品也將加入 EML，
營運成長動能顯著，因此給予 40xPER，目標價 680 元，建議區
間操作。  
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
華星光(4979)成立於 2001 年，為國內光通訊主動元件生產廠，產品
包含雷射晶粒、次模組、光收發模組代工。2025 年光通訊主動元件
及模組佔 86%、晶粒 11%、其他 3%，銷售地區則以北美為主，佔
87%、台灣 11%、其他 2%。生產基地在中壢合江廠與吉林廠，預
計新增竹東和八德廠。

伴隨雷射客戶需求出貨，26H1 晶粒營收 Y+69% 
華星光 26Q2 營收 12.9 億元 ，Q+8.0%，Y+18.5%， 毛利率22.44%，
稅後淨利 1.8 億元 ，Q-19.8%，Y+94.7%，EPS 1.29 元。26H1 400G 
ZR/ZR+需求穩定出貨，營收成長動能主要來自於晶粒產品，隨著雷
射客戶需求的成長，華星光 26H1 晶粒營收 Y+69%。然而由於財稅
認列時點影響毛利率，Q-2.98ppts，預計 Q3 會恢復提列。

800G ZR/ZR+放量貢獻，竹東廠加入投產  
華星光 7 月營收 5.1 億元 ，M+11.8%，Y+31.0%， 其中 ，400G ZR/ZR+
出貨維持穩定，800G ZR/ZR+則逐步進入放量成長階段，帶動光收
發模組代工業務成為新的營運成長動能。另一方面，因應雷射客戶
持續擴充產能，公司同步加大無塵室投資，竹東廠預計於 26Q3 加
入營運，進一步擴充整體生產量能，支撐後續雷射相關產品需求成
長 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估華星光26Q3 營收 16.7 億元 ，Q+29.8%，
Y+55.5% ，毛利率 27.83% ，稅後淨利 3.4 億元， Q+82.7% ，
Y+54.6%，EPS 2.36 元。

2026 年底晶粒產能翻倍，2027 年 DCI 產品貢獻翻倍 
根據 Yole 統計 ，2025 年全球 400G ZR/ZR+需求量約 850 萬支 ，2026
年預估增至 1,000 萬支 ，2027 年則維持約 975 萬支水準 ； 另一方面 ，
800G ZR/ZR+ 開始進入快速成長期，2026 年出貨量預估約 700 萬
支，2027 年將進一步增至 1,000 萬支，並有望於 2028 年後逐步成
為 DCI 市場主流規格，帶動高速光模組需求持續升級。雷射產品亦
同步朝高功率方向發展，目前主流功率約 70/100mW，未來將逐步
提升至 400mW 以上，高功率產品所需晶片面積亦將顯著增加，進
一步推升晶圓與後段封裝產能需求。因應客戶需求成長，公司持續
擴充產能 ，2025 年底整體無塵室樓地板面積約 6,550 平方米 ，2026

年 EPS 7.95 元，2027 年 EPS 16.99 元，目前 PE 為 36x，參考國
內光通訊類股 PE 區間為 20-50x， 考量下一世代800G ZR/ZR+產品
將開始加入貢獻，另外雷射後段產品也將加入 EML，營運成長動能
顯著，因此給予 40xPER，目標價 680 元，建議區間操作。

### Page 1

審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

宜鼎(5289) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：價值取勝策略搭配較佳客戶結構，長期獲利成長
性遠勝同業

一 、 宜鼎為工控記憶體模組大廠 ， 工業SSD 全球市占第
一 
宜鼎 100%營收皆來自於非消費性業務，以 InnoDisk 品
牌行銷全球 ， 工控SSD 全球市占第一 ， 並為全球前十大
DRAM 模組供應商，就產品結構而言，Flash、DRAM、
AI Solution 和其他分別占 1H26 營收比重為 32%、64%、
4%，因 Flash 產品客製化產品較高 ， 毛利率優於DRAM
產品。就營收區域而言，台灣、歐洲、美洲、中國、亞
洲 、 和其他分別占1H26 營收比重為 26%、14%、18%、
20%、21%和 1%。宜鼎於工控領域國內競爭對手包含
創見、宇瞻、威剛。因記憶體廠商競爭門檻在於料源取
得以及通路佈建 ， 宜鼎在料源部分與日美韓廠皆有合作 ，
且工控通路佈局綿密，客戶超過四千家顯現營收來源相
當分散。宜蘭二廠於 2024 年七月啟用，主要生產 AI 
Camera 和 AI 相關周邊，2026 年產能 1080K，最大產
能為 1440K。 
二、2Q26 EPS 108.93 元創新高並優於預期，3Q26 獲
利可望維持高檔  
2Q26 營收 224.4 億元(+70%QoQ)優於預期之 204.5 億
元，其中以 Flash 產品成長性較佳，毛利率 65%優於前
季之 59.1%，營業利益 131.46 億元(+92%QoQ)優於預
估值 21%，業外主因匯兌認列收益 0.69 億元，稅後純
益 103.68 億元(+90%QoQ)，EPS 108.93 元創新高並優
於預期之 89.95 元。展望 3Q26，客戶需求持續成長，
且料源獲得原廠支持，估計 3Q26 營收 258.6 億元
(+16%QoQ)， 因低價庫存利益降低 ， 毛利率 55%低於上
季 65%，稅後純益 102.1 億元(-2%QoQ)，EPS 107.27
元。 
三、產業供不應求將延續至 2027 年，公司為原廠產能
優先分配對象 
CSP 業者持續擴大投資 AI 相關基礎建設，集邦預估
2026 年和 2027 年全球九大 CSP 資本支出分別為 8,867
億美元(+90%YoY)和 1.32 兆美元(+49%YoY)，2026 年
AI 相關需求已經成為記憶體產業最大單一應用需求，主
要原廠亦將 AI 相關 Server 應用需求列為產能優先分配
對象，產業供不應求以及資源排擠效應，致使記憶體價
格自 4Q25-2Q26 連三季季漲幅超過 50%，預期產業供
不應求將延續至 2027 年。宜鼎客戶聚焦 AI 基礎建設、
全球垂直應用品牌以及高附加價值工控解決方案供應商
等三大類型，除客戶成本耐受能力高外，且此三類型客
戶亦為原廠產能優先支援對象，此為今年前七月營收
+549%YoY 成長性領先國內模組廠主因。 
四、1H26 AI 占比提升至 40%，高附加價值解決方案為
長期獲利成長來源 
AI 基礎建設已切入全球雲端服務供應商資料中心 、AI 加
速卡、前幾大光通訊與網路交換器客戶，Edge AI 趨勢
有助於記憶體搭載量提升，涵蓋人形機器人等物理 AI
以及企業級邊緣 AI 應用 ， 公司整合Storage、Memory、
Sensor、周邊系統、加速卡以及 AI Platform、韌體與管
理軟體給不同需求類型 Edge AI 客戶群，1H26 Edge AI
營收占比已達 40%遠優於先前預期之全年 30%目標 ， 故
公司獲利成長不僅僅是受惠於低價庫存，而是在於其 AI
拓展有成，以及其解決方案對於客戶之高附加價值。 
五、宜蘭三廠擴建預計 2028 年完成，既有產能將可支
應未來兩年成長  
為因應客戶長線需求，宜鼎將進行宜蘭三廠擴建，擴建
計畫包含地上八層以及地下兩層的三廠以及員工宿舍，
產能規模將超越目前宜蘭一廠以及二廠總和，4Q26 將
開始動工，並預計 2028 年完工投產，目前宜蘭二廠產
能預計仍可滿足未來兩年客戶需求，故不會有產能不足
問題。 
六、維持買進理由 
1)預估 2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 375.31 元和
388.33 元。 
2)考量價值取勝策略搭配較佳客戶結構，長期獲利成長
性遠勝同業，維持買進評等，TP 2100 元 (2027 PB x 
3)。

## Document 118: 5289宜鼎 國票

Source PDF: all report pdf/5289宜鼎 國票.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-30
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

請參閱末頁之聲明

2026.08.28 
陳靖元 C00190@ibfs.com.tw 
5289.TT 宜鼎 
 需求仍然強勁且獲利結構穩定

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
宜鼎為工業級嵌入式儲存與記憶體解決方案領導廠商 。 公司深耕工業控制 、 航太國防 、
醫療及智慧交通等應用市場，產品以高可靠度、長生命週期及耐極端環境為標準，目
前全球客戶數超過 4,000 家，營運模式以高毛利、小量多樣的客製化服務為核心。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 2,080 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，1,780 
【大盤指數/股價】45975 / 1490

➢ 2Q26 受惠 Flash 報價上漲，帶動營收獲利成長 
2Q26 營收 224.43 億元，QoQ+70.3%，YoY+641.1%，推估 2Q26 
DRAM / SSD ASP 分別上漲 40% / 80%，出貨量則季增逾 10%。宜鼎
除了與原廠確保產能之外，同時也有增加供應商並簽訂 LTA，得以在需
求強勁下維持穩定庫存水位以及相對較低的庫存成本，故 2Q26 毛利率
65%，EPS 為 108.93 元，QoQ+89.7%，YoY+5,493.8%。  
➢ 3Q26 預期 ASP 仍具上漲動能 
展望 3Q26，預期 3Q26 DRAM/SSD 將分別季增 20% / 40%，出貨量
方面，預估 3Q26 出貨量將較 2Q26 呈現微幅季減。預估 3Q26 營收約
274.81 億元，QoQ+22.4%，YoY+621.8%。此外，受惠於貨源穩定，
加上大型客戶對於 DRAM 及 SSD 的終端需求表現依舊強勁，故宜鼎仍
可維持強勁獲利表現，預估 3Q26 毛利率約 65.5%，EPS 為 134 元，
QoQ+18.3%，YoY+1,808.1%。  
➢ 宜鼎貨源充足，預期獲利能力相對穩定 
考量 2H26 報價仍有望延續漲勢，加上網通與美、中系CSP 客戶拉貨動
能強勁，預估 2026 年營收約 923.89 億元 ，YoY+547.8%， 毛利率63.8%，
EPS 為 434.8 元，YoY+1,885.2%。預估 2027 年營收為 989.8 億元，
YoY+7.1%， 雖然仍預期DRAM/SSD 報價維持高檔不墜 ， 然而考量記憶
體供需缺口將進一步放大，恐使出貨量較 2026 年呈現收斂。預估 2027
年毛利率約為 57.2%，EPS 為 415.58 元，YoY-5.8%。 
➢ 看好記憶體趨勢不變，且網通、伺服器需求強勁，維持買進建議 
整體而言 ， 宜鼎受惠於記憶體報價上漲 ，2026 年營收 、 獲利皆大幅成長 ，
宜鼎不僅已向原廠簽訂 LTA 確保後續產能，且其客群多為網通、工控客
戶，較不容易因漲價而降低採購量，宜鼎具備議價能力。而 2027 年仍
預期記憶體供不應求態勢延續，安提的工廠自動化、機器人專案放量，
亦將優化整體獲利結構，同時挹注中長期營運動能。評價方面，考量宜
鼎貨源充足，且受惠客戶屬性，獲利結構相對穩定，再加上 2H26 報價
仍具上漲動能，維持買進建議，目標價 2,080 元(2027F PE 5X)。 
近一年個股及大盤報酬率

公司基本資料  
流通在外股數(百萬股) 98 
市值(百萬元) 146,511

5289 宜鼎 加權指數

### Page 2

個股報告

公司簡介 
宜鼎國際成立於 2005 年，為全球工業級嵌入式儲存與記憶體解決方案領導廠商。公
司長期深耕工業控制 、 航太國防 、 醫療及智慧交通等垂直應用市場 ， 產品以高可靠度 、
長生命週期及耐極端環境為核心標準，目前全球企業客戶數超過 4,000 家，營運模式
以高毛利 、 小量多樣的客製化服務為核心 。 公司透過旗下子公司安堤切入邊緣AI 運算
市場。安堤作為 NVIDIA Elite 級合作夥伴，專注於整合運算架構與 AI 軟體，母公司
則提供底層記憶體與儲存支持。此垂直整合模式使宜鼎從單純的零組件供應商，成功
轉型為具備軟硬體整合能力的 Edge AI 系統方案商」應用範疇已擴展至自動導引車
(AGV)、自主移動機器人及智慧監控等高附加價值領域。 
圖 1：宜鼎產品組合

資料來源：公司,國票投顧整理 
2Q26 受惠 Flash 報價上漲，帶動營收獲利成長 
2Q26 營收 224.43 億元，QoQ+70.3%，YoY+641.1%，主要仍受惠於記憶體供需失
衡導致報價持續上漲，且網通/工控客戶庫存水位相對較低，拉貨力道相對強勁，推估
2Q26 DRAM / SSD ASP 分別上漲 40%/80%，出貨量則季增逾 10%。獲利表現方面 ，
受惠於報價調漲，且 SSD 營收占比提升，再加上開機碟開始出貨予資料中心，整體產
品組合優化 ， 此外 ，宜鼎除了與原廠確保產能之外 ，同時也有增加供應商並簽訂 LTA，
得以在需求強勁下維持穩定庫存水位以及相對較低的庫存成本 ，故 2Q26 毛利率
65%，QoQ+5.9ppts，YoY+39.1ppts，稅後淨利 103.68 億元， QoQ+89.7% ，
YoY+5,493.8%，EPS 為 108.93 元。 
3Q26 預期 ASP 仍具上漲動能 
展望 3Q26，考量原廠持續漲價，預期 3Q26 DRAM / SSD 將分別季增 20% / 40%，
公司將逐月反映報價以轉嫁成本予客戶，出貨量方面，雖然網通、伺服器客戶訂單能
見度仍然強勁 ，惟工控客戶在記憶體大幅漲價， 以及CPU 缺貨的影響下，拉貨動能略
微放緩，故預估 3Q26 出貨量將較 2Q26 呈現微幅季減。預估 3Q26 營收約 274.81
億元 ，QoQ+22.4%，YoY+621.8%。此外，受惠於貨源穩定 ， 加上大型客戶對於DRAM
及 SSD 的終端需求表現依舊強勁，故宜鼎仍可維持強勁獲利表現，預估 3Q26 毛利
率約 65.5%，QoQ+0.5ppts，YoY+32.7ppts， 稅後淨利為122.7 億元 ，QoQ+18.3%，
YoY+1,808.1%，EPS 為 134 元。

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
宜鼎貨源充足，預期獲利能力相對穩定 
展望 2026 年，宜鼎將從單純的模組供應商轉型為 Edge AI 系統整合商。營運重心除
傳統工控外，美、中系CSP 客戶的邊緣運算專案將成為核心動能。從報價來看，預期
3Q26、4Q26 仍有望延續漲勢，加上網通與美、中系 CSP 客戶拉貨動能強勁，且子
公司安提的 AGV 與工廠自動化機器人等專案也已經自 1Q26 陸續開始出貨。投研部
預估 2026 年 DRAM 與 SSD 的出貨量皆可望年增逾 60%。此外，由於公司將持續向
客戶調漲報價以轉嫁成本，故 DRAM/SSD ASP 有望年增逾 200%。DRAM 產能方
面，宜鼎已經與韓廠確保全年穩定產能配貨，同時亦與台廠簽訂 LTA，以確保產能充
足；Flash 方面 ，宜鼎同樣與原廠談妥長期訂單 ， 確保部分利基型客戶在面臨產線EOL
時，仍有穩定供貨。而在客戶結構上，宜鼎亦成功切入美系存儲供應鏈，CSP 客戶營
收占比提升。此外，子公司安提在 Edge AI 專案已經邁入量產，營收自 2Q26 倍數成
長，預期 2026 年營收可望成長逾 200%。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收約 923.89 億
元，YoY+547.8%，毛利率 63.8%，YoY+32.7ppts，稅後淨利為 404.12 億元，
YoY+1,885.2%，EPS 為 434.8 元。 
投研部預估 2027 年營收為 989.8 億元，YoY+7.1%，雖然仍預期 DRAM / SSD 報價
維持高檔不墜，然而考量記憶體供需缺口將進一步放大， 恐使出貨量較 2026 年呈現
收斂；子公司安提方面，預期 2027 年仍將維持強勁成長動能，預期在新一代自動化
機器人專案量產貢獻下，營收可望年增逾 60%。獲利表現方面，考量公司產品終端應
用客戶多為工控、伺服器、網通等客戶，其價格敏感度相對較低，且預期拉貨動能仍
然強勁，再加上高毛利專案貢獻下，預期毛利率仍可維持相對高檔，預估 2027 年毛
利率約為 57.2%，YoY-6.6ppts，稅後淨利為 380.56 億元 ，YoY-5.8%，EPS 為 415.58
元。 
在產能規劃上，為了支持未來 Edge AI 市場的成長，公司規劃於宜蘭科學園區研發製
造中心興建三廠。預計於 4Q26 動工，並規劃在 2028 年完全落成啟用並投入產出。
在此之前，現有一廠與二廠的產能已預留充足空間，足以 支撐 2026 至 2027 年的營
運動能，確保宜鼎持續憑藉其軟硬體一站式整合的高附加價值與利基優勢，維持穩固
的地位。 
看好記憶體趨勢不變，且網通、伺服器需求強勁，維持買進建議 
整體而言 ，宜鼎受惠於記憶體報價上漲 ，2026 年營收 、 獲利皆大幅成長 ，且觀察SSD
需求隨著推論所需儲存空間不斷增加 ， 預計將維持供不應求態勢 ， 即便2027 年、2028
年原廠新增產能， 惟其大多將多數產能集中於DRAM，故短期內供需緊張態勢將難以
緩解。宜鼎不僅已向原廠簽訂 LTA 確保後續產能，且其客群多為網通、工控客戶，較
不容易因漲價而降低採購量，宜鼎具備議價能力，故即便庫存成本上升，仍預期宜鼎
毛利率仍可維持相對高檔。而 2027 年仍預期記憶體供不應求態勢延續，安提的工廠
自動化、機器人專案放量，亦將優化整體獲利結構，同時挹注中長期營運動能。評價
方面，過往本益比區間約為 3X~18X，目前本益比約為 3.6X，位於區間下緣，考量宜
鼎貨源充足 ， 且受惠客戶屬性 ， 獲利結構相對穩定 ， 再加上2H26 報價仍具上漲動能 ，
維持買進建議，目標價 2,080 元(2027F PE 5X)。

陳鈞皓 C00264@ibfs.com.tw 
6190.TT 萬泰科 
 營運谷底已過 營運逐季走揚

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
萬泰科技股份有限公司成立於 1978 年 6 月，目前主要產品包括區域網路線、光纖光
纜、電子線、RF 高頻同軸線、電源線等產品。終端產品大致可分為網路線、電子線、
車用線、灌溉線、輻照線、電源線、電腦線、高溫線、其他等類別。  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 90 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】 買 進 ，105 
【大盤指數/股價】46949 / 71.6

➢ 2Q26 為營運谷底 獲利率仍受原物料所影響 
回顧 2Q26，營收為 29.79 億元，較預期的 28.41 億元高了 4.8%。毛利
率為 15.3%較預期的 15.8%低，主因中東戰爭導致原物料包括石油跟化
學塑膠價格上漲 ， 塑化產品不同於銅價格能隨著LME 報價上漲而反映至
客戶 ， 因此塑化材料的上漲較難反映成本給客戶 ， 導致短期毛利率承壓 。
費用則為 2.38 億元，較預期的 2.61 億元低，顯示費用管控得當。EPS 
0.92 元比預期的 0.81 元高，主因稅率大幅下滑至 18%，較預期的 29%
低，主因海外基地都面臨廠房擴建需求當中，需要大量的資金，因此短
期內不會將獲利匯回台灣，導致所得稅下滑。2Q26 營收 29.79 億元，
QoQ+19.57% ， YoY+32.84% 。 EPS 0. 92 元， QoQ+113.62% ，
YoY+159.92%。 
➢ 2H26 獲利率改善 營運逐季走揚 
展望 2H26，低軌衛星方面出貨量持續上修，2025 年出貨總套數約 200
萬套，1Q26 已出貨 150 萬套，2Q26 成長至 220 套，預估 3Q~4Q 之
出貨量仍呈現季度之上升 ， 預估2026 全年出貨套數將超過 1,000 萬套 ，
達到 2026 年低軌業務營收佔比 6%~7%之目標。獲利率方面，面對材
料成本上漲，公司已在 2Q 與客戶進行價格的調整與談判，已在 6 月開
始反映在財報上，因此預估 3Q26 的毛利率狀況將校 2Q26 大幅改善，
毛利率 3Q26~4Q26 有望回升至 2025 年 17%左右之水準 。研究員預估
3Q26 營收 30.24 億元，QoQ+1.5%，YoY+47.48%。EPS 1.31 元，
QoQ+27.58%，YoY+128.81%。 
➢ AI 線材及低軌衛星成長趨勢明確 維持買進投資建議 
展望後市，AI 線材趨勢成長明確，規格提升帶動 ASP 持續有 30%增長
之機會。而低軌衛星除了既有的 AC 電源線及網路線外，目前已與客戶
共同開發，期望能用在衛星上的太陽能線，衛星訊號傳輸線等，具有長
線想像空間。研究員估計 2026 年/2027 年營收 111.99 億元/141.22 億
元，YoY+29.71% / +26.1%，EPS 4.24 元/6.07 元，YoY+70.16% / 
58.22%。過往評價區間約位於 13X~22X 之間，目前評價則在 12 倍左
右，考量當前評價仍有提升空間，給予目標價90 元(2027F PE 15X)，建
議買進。

個股報告

公司簡介 
萬泰科技股份有限公司成立於 1978 年 6 月，目前主要產品包括區域網路線、光纖光
纜、電子線、RF 高頻同軸線、電源線等產品。終端產品應用大致可分為網路線、電子
線、車用線、灌溉線、輻照線、電源線、電腦線、其他等類別，2025 年營收佔比分別
約為 34%、28%、9%、5%、6%、2%、1%、17%。產能布局方面，當前廠房包括東
莞廠、越南廠、泰國廠等廠房，東莞專注於開發鐵路相關線材，並積極開發船舶、醫
療、機器人及機械手臂用線。越南廠則以承接衛星通訊網路線訂單為主，另接手泰國
廠的灌溉用線及輻照射備用線。泰國廠當前則以發展 AI 高速傳輸線(PCIe 5、PCIe 6、
112G)為主。新產品開發方面，將針對 PCIe 7.0 及 112G PAM4 應用的高速傳輸內部
連接線材進行開發，並優化頻率來確保訊號的穩定性。另將針對極端低溫環境開發 -
70℃之耐候電纜，適用於戶外嚴苛環境。

2Q26 為營運谷底 獲利率仍受原物料所影響 
回顧 2Q26，營收為 29.79 億元，較預期的 28.41 億元高了 4.8%。毛利率為 15.3%較
預期的 15.8%低，主因中東戰爭導致原物料包括石油跟化學塑膠價格上漲，塑化產品
不同於銅價格能隨著 LME 報價上漲而反映至客戶 ， 因此塑化材料的上漲較難反映成本
給客戶，導致短期毛利率承壓。費用則為 2.38 億元，較預期的 2.61 億元低，顯示費
用管控得當。EPS 0.92 元比預期的 0.81 元高，主因稅率大幅下滑至 18%，較預期的
29%低，主因海外基地都面臨廠房擴建需求當中，需要大量的資金，因此短期內不會
將 獲 利 匯 回 台 灣 ， 導 致 所 得 稅 下 滑 。2Q26 營收 29.79 億 元 ，QoQ+19.57% ，
YoY+32.84%。EPS 0.92 元，QoQ+113.62%，YoY+159.92%。 
2H26 獲利率改善 營運逐季走揚 
展望 2H26， 在需求強勁的AI 高速線材方面 ， 目前泰國第一期月產能200 萬米產線之
稼動率處於滿載之狀況 ，且泰國第二期產能於2026/04 開始慢慢提升稼動率後 ，目前
高速線的產能以達到月產能 1,000 萬米的目標 。在產能最大的第三期產線已開始動工 ，
投入的資本支出約在 2.5 億元，預估會在 2027 年 4~5 月啟用，並在 2027 年底或是
2028 年達到 2,000 萬米月產能的目標。目前 AI 線材的規格以 PCIe 6.0 為主，相對

PCIe 5.0 有 30%的 ASP 提升，而目前正在研發的 PCIe 7.0 相對 PCIe 6.0 又有 ASP 
30%的提升，帶動產品組合及獲利率持續轉佳，目前 PCIe 7.0 已完成產品的開發，並
配合客戶進行更細節的開發結構認證。低軌衛星方面出貨量持續上修 ，2025 年出貨總
套數約 200 萬套，1Q26 已出貨 150 萬套，2Q26 成長至 220 套，預估 3Q~4Q 之出
貨量仍呈現季度之上升，預估 2026 全年出貨套數將超過 1,000 萬套，達到 2026 年
低軌業務營收佔比 6%~7%之目標 。 獲利率方面 ，面對材料成本上漲 ， 公司已在2Q 與
客戶進行價格的調整與談判，已在 6 月開始反映在財報上，因此預估 3Q26 的毛利率
狀況將校 2Q26 大幅改善，毛利率 3Q26~4Q26 有望回升至 2025 年 17%左右之水
準。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 30.24 億元，QoQ+1.5%，YoY+47.48%。EPS 1.31 元，
QoQ+27.58%，YoY+128.81%。 
2026 年為 AI 高速線材及低軌帶動公司營運成長 
展望 2026~2027 年，高毛利率之傳輸線材仍為主要成長動能，包括 AI、低軌衛星及
美國基建等終端應用。AI 伺服器業務方面，公司已在泰國布局 3 階段之產能擴充，目
前第一期之產能約 200 萬米/月，已處於滿載之狀況。二期產能則在3M/26 擴廠完成
後逐月拉高稼動率 ，目前已能提供月產能 1,000 萬米之需求。 三期產能約為800~1,000
萬米/月左右 ， 預計在2Q27 擴廠完成 ， 並在2H27 開始貢獻營收 。 規格方面預期2026
年的 AI 線材規格將從過往的 PCIe 5.0 轉往 PCIe 6.0，並已完成 PCIe 7.0 的研發，而
規格的提升也帶動售價大幅成長，在二期及三期產能逐漸開出之情況下，價量將同步
成長。研究員估計 2026 年 AI 線材之營收佔比約達到 3%上下，2027 年進一步提升
至高個位數之營收占比。且 AI 線材之毛利率介於 25％~30%之間，明顯優於公司平
均的 16％~17%，將帶動產品組合轉佳，進一步提升獲利能力。 
低軌衛星業務方面，主要出貨接收站與路由器之間相連的網路線及路由器之 AC 電源
線，在 AC 電源線方面為主要之供應廠商，根據 SpaceX 官方公告，目前之全球訂閱
數已達 1,200 萬戶，其訂閱戶數在 2024 年底僅 460 萬戶，約一年多時間，訂閱戶數
達翻倍之成長，根據研調機構資料顯示， 2026 年 底 之 訂 閱 戶 數 有 機 會 成 長 至
1,400~1,500 萬之間 。 另SpaceX 期望在 2026 年底能讓行動端用戶突破 2,500 萬人 ，
可見其成長動能極為強勁。每組 Starlink 套件約在 349~599 美元之間，因用戶須購
買套件才能啟用該服務，因此硬體設備之銷售量與訂閱數密切相關，估計低軌營收
2025 年之佔比約在 3%上下，並在 2026 年達到 6~7%之營收佔比，為公司成長最為
強勁之產品線。 
AI 線材及低軌衛星成長趨勢明確 維持買進投資建議 
展望後市，AI 線材趨勢成長明確，規格提升帶動 ASP 持續有 30%增長之機會。而低
軌衛星除了既有的 AC 電源線及網路線外，目前已與客戶共同開發，期望能用在衛星
上的太陽能線 ， 衛星訊號傳輸線等 ， 具有長線想像空間 。 研究員估計2026 年/2027 年
營收 111.99 億元/141.22 億元，YoY+29.71% /+26.1%，EPS 4.24 元/6.07 元，
YoY+70.16% / 58.22%。過往評價區間約位於 13X~22X 之間，目前評價則在 12 倍
左右，考量當前評價仍有提升空間，給予目標價 90 元(2027F PE 15X)，建議買進。

林秋香 
886-2-27815995#37026 
mandy.cs.lin@fubon.com 
兆聯實業 (6944 TT)

高科技產業擴廠帶動公司營運續揚

投資評等維持買進，目標價 976 元，潛在漲幅 37% 
1. 毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
2. 在手訂單續創新高 
3. 因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長

毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
兆聯實業 2Q26 營收為 50.8 億元，YoY+36.1%，略低於預期。其中工程類營收為
39.3 億元(YoY+35.0%)，占比 77%，主要係因在手工程持續去化；服務類營收為
11.5 億元(YoY+40.3%)， 成長轉趨強勁主要係因2H25 開始有一個新的製程使用新
的耗材所致，占比 23%。受惠於 1)美國專案工程執行效率佳，完工進度較預期時間
短、2)新承做專案因業主急於擴廠，對施工進度的考量甚於成本，故公司的接單毛
利率較高，與 3)去年接單時台幣較為強勢，今年台幣趨貶，故 2Q26 工程毛利率上
揚至 28%( vs. 1Q26 的 26.5%)，服務類毛利率則達 30.1% (vs. 1Q26 的 29.9%)，
帶動整體毛利率達 28.4%，優於預期。因此，稅後淨利達 8.7 億元，YoY+70.8%，
亦高於研究部預期。若以除權後股本計算，EPS 為 8.75 元。

在手訂單續創新高 
受惠於 AI 需求強勁，半導體、記憶體、PCB、ABF 產業建廠積極度高，兆聯實業 6
月底在手訂單餘額達 277 億元，分別為國內 73%、美國 25%、新加坡 7%、中國
<5%。公司並於 7 月發布重訊公告接獲美國 50 億元以上訂單，若含此訂單，美國
占比將提升至 40%。 由於在手訂單續創新高 ， 研究部正向看待未來工程類營收成長 ，
並預估 3Q26 營收為 59.6 億元，YoY+24.5%。基於 3Q26 工程類毛利率可望持平
於 2Q26 之假設，預估稅後淨利為 10.4 億元，YoY+51.9%，EPS 為 10.4 元。

因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長 
兆聯實業近期公告將發行兩檔無擔保轉換公司債，合計金額為新台幣 25 億元，主
要用於支應美國營運資金需求 ， 預估此對股權稀釋比例約3%。 因應客戶建廠需求 ，
為取得資本財與服務財優勢，公司於台灣中南部有購置土地或建廠計畫，資本支出
約 10 億元；海外方面，公司將投入 19.5 億元建置美國新廠(第一期)，此廠可望在
2028 年完工。雖然業主隨著發包案量提升而有培養其他供應商的計畫，然因兆聯
實業已與業主合作多年，建廠配合默契佳，與國際競爭者相較亦 有成本費用優勢，
故公司競爭性仍佳。除了優化工程進度以提升毛利率外，公司目前已切入記憶體、
先進封裝、載板的純水系統，因純水系統的毛利率較高，未來若能切入半導體製造
廠的純水系統，則毛利率將再提升，另外，美國新廠完成後，公司服務類營收將加
速成長，毛利率亦將隨之提升。

富邦不同之處 
富邦 2026/2027 EPS(F) 為 38.54/48.79 元，Bloomberg 預估為 37.74/41.11 元。

評價與風險 
兆聯實業聚焦在高科技廠水資源工程，因半導體大客戶先進製程擴展不停歇，將帶
動業績再創新高。評價風險為半導體產業擴張放緩，以及原料與人工成本飆漲。
